		"npc_dota_hero_dragon_knight_lore"			"他诞生于原始火焰的核心，伴随着永恒之火的愤怒。他的熔岩盔甲和宝剑指引着他，去完成他应该执行的伟大审判：杀死巨龙沃瑟姆，并撬出它的心脏。当他追寻巨龙到达猩红山脉之后，他的真正力量终于苏醒了。他浑身爆发出炽热的烈火，使巨龙也为之眩目，随后用他的宝剑摧枯拉朽的将巨龙斩杀。 他手中攥着巨龙的心脏，只用目光一瞥就将其彻底焚烧殆尽。 当他重返诞生之地时，惊奇的发现那儿有一把小刀。这把刀嵌在巨石之中，刀柄上用铭文镌刻着一个名字：“烈焰行者”。"
		"npc_dota_hero_phantom_assassin_lore"			"她由纯粹的能量组成，甚至传说她就是最初点燃宇宙的火光。虽然听起来她是来自远古的本源，但她实在远非如此。她是一个拥有远超自身理解之能力的天真女孩，她的刀锋中蕴含着肆虐破坏的电流力量。她天生就有轻抖手腕就能操纵原子电荷、并随时调用宇宙中自然电力的能力。然而，这一切对她来说都不重要了，因为她一直渴望的只是找到她多年前失去的爱。"
		"npc_dota_hero_necrolyte_lore"					"有这样一个曾经充满好奇心的小男孩进行了无数冒险，而现在他已是一个被自己的好奇心抛弃的老隐士。在被一条毒蛇咬过一口之后，他在塔纳魔森林最深的灌木丛中呼吸苦难的挣扎着。在他勉强拖着自己的身子回到村子时，一个萨满在他家门口迎接他。这个男孩决定跟随这个浑身覆盖着闪着绿光纹身的巫师。巫师把他带回了那个遍布毒素的地方，他信奉的神殿殿。在那里男孩要做出一个抉择：保留他身上的毒液然后成为毒物之一，或是在丛林中被治愈后将他流放。男孩选择了毒液这也意味着选择了死亡。他为了保持意识苦苦挣扎着，他的身体上出现了类似那个萨满的纹身，接着一条蛇从沼泽中升起。蛇变得坚硬了接着变成了一个叫做枯萎毒液的武器。男孩变老了且独自一人徘徊在生死边缘。现在他就是传染病本身，是瘟疫的活生生的体现。"
		"npc_dota_hero_axe_lore"							"在他小的时候，他被父母遗留在红雾军团控制下的一个村庄里。他和另一个被遗弃的男孩一起长大，他把他称作兄弟。他照顾他们两个人，并且认为自己是领导者和保护者。他总觉得生活是残酷和不公平的，所以他把这种哲学应用到阻挡他的任何竞争对手身上。随着年龄的增长，他通过不断的浴血战斗并且获得胜利赢得了他人的尊重。他成了一个无人可挡的机器，只知道得到奖励的唯一途径就是破坏。在那种盲目的愤怒和战斗的荣耀中，他杀死了他最亲爱的人，他唯一的朋友，他的兄弟。那一刻，他的皮肤变成了红色，他兄弟的血永远的成为了他的标志。当士兵看到他所做的事情时，他们恐惧他的力量。他成了战士们的领袖，他成了赤色上将"
		"npc_dota_hero_drow_ranger_lore"					 "莫安娜只是一个来自林中小村的普通女孩。每天她和她的兄弟们都会去树林里练习箭法。尽管他们的母亲警告他们黑暗充满了无尽的危险，但他们仍然会在森林的夜里待到很晚。他们从不听劝阻，直到一天晚上，满月在夜空中高高挂起，Crimsyth冲了进来破坏并掌控了森林。怪物的涌入使得莫安娜和她的兄弟们迷路了，找不到回家的路，因此她们不得不在幼小的年纪想尽一切办法活下来。希望的曙光似乎永远不会到来，在接下来的几个星期里，她眼睁睁地看着她的兄弟们被属于黑夜的生物无情地杀害。在森林里经历了60天的黑暗之后，莫安娜孤独而又恐惧，她跪倒在地，抬头仰望夜空，再一次看到了那个充满光芒的圆月。她双手合十，无助地向她的兄弟以及被她忽略的母亲道歉。月光照耀着她满是泪水的脸颊，月亮的能量注入了她的身体和弓箭。现在她能运用来自月亮和星星的祝福并且发誓用这份力量来保护她所珍视的一切。."
		"npc_dota_hero_obsidian_destroyer_lore"			"即使是在我们的宇宙被创造之前，就有一些以永恒的时间流动具现化形式而存在的生命。 Astrius是其中一个实体，被称为时光守卫。在起源事件发生的数十亿年后，Astrius对重复的时间一成不变越发感到厌倦。他徘徊在各个维度中寻找着禁忌之星。在不知道会发生什么的情况下，他走进了星球内部，在那里他的时间能量被吞噬。亿万年之后，Astrius发现自己和其他凡人一起在陆地上行走。尽管他仍然保持着掌控时间的能力，但他发现与他旧时的力量相比自己受到了严重限制。但他却十分满足，因为生与死已经为他的永恒存在赋予了新的意义，这足以让他兴奋不已。."
		"npc_dota_hero_omniknight_lore"					"在太阳深处，一个强大的人被正义之名召唤而来。 光明的半神索利斯想要祝福一个能够为他光芒的代理，并且用圣火净化邪恶的人。这个人必须足够强大以实现他的意志，但也必须足够纯洁毫无罪恶。在没有通知他的信徒们的情况下，索利斯任由邪恶在他神圣的寺庙附近壮大肆虐。那些软弱的人认为索利斯背叛了他们，这些人开始背弃光明。正是这个时期造就了黑暗时代。然而有一个特殊的信徒并没有放弃信仰屈服于邪恶，他全心全意地发誓要摧毁邪恶。侵占寺庙的黑暗之灵十分强大，但这个信徒凭借着他闪耀的品格和圣洁的心灵，终于打败了邪恶。当他的敌人一个个倒在他身下时，他心中充满了胜利之光。他找到了索利斯的强大的圣锤，在那一刻他感受到了前所未有的力量。他成为了代表正义的光辉灯塔，现在他以索利斯之圣名摧毁邪恶。."  
		"npc_dota_hero_crystal_maiden_lore"				"安里是个神童。曾经大巫师协会里的一名法师，她是世界上最强大的魔法师之一。她拥有完全掌握和控制冰与火的能力，她可以毫不费力地引导出奥术能量，就如走路一般轻松。在她探求知识与力量的过程中，在不知道会带来何种风险的情况下，她开始尝试暗能量。她的学徒对她在深夜失踪感到怀疑，并潜入她的塔楼，一个在门扉处有强大能量肆虐的塔楼。混乱的绿色能量肆意涌动，安里猝不及防，她因窥探的学徒而感到恼怒。她开始疯狂地念诵咒语，并从她的法杖里发射了出去，直逼门扉。它坍塌了，但是一团混乱的能量反弹并注入了安里体内。大巫师协会认定，安里的行为是不可原谅的，黑暗之门的腐败能量是对和平的威胁。安里不再被信任她也因此被驱逐出协会。她因羞耻和混乱带来的后果而伤痕累累，现在她在凛冬枯霜山脉的废墟里磨练她的神奇力量."
		"npc_dota_hero_invoker_lore"						"The conjuror started out as a regular student at the Academy of Elements. He was a promising young man, but always showed an interest in forbidden magic. His mentors began to worry about him, as he was showing signs of a young psychopath who cared about nothing but magic. As his knowledge grew he became ever more arrogant and narcissistic. Through each year that he graduated he delved deeper and deeper into dangerous and evil spellcasting theories. The Academy expelled him to try and teach him a lesson, but it made no difference. Conjuration was where he found his true calling. An art befitting a maddened prodigy it only grew stronger as he practiced day and night, knowing no rest. He read texts from any origin and practiced conjurations that nobody had ever conceived of. Finally he reached a pinnacle of genius. Through the mixture of dark arts and elemental mastery he had perfected his craft. He can now summon corporeal avatars composed of pure elements that are completely under his command."
		"npc_dota_hero_juggernaut_lore"					 "Seinaru is a distinguished member of the Sunwarrior clan, a master of the art of Blade Dancing, and conqueror of the Valley of a Thousand Suns. Seinaru is one of the fastest known warriors without using magic. On days where he is not in battle, he has a strict training regimen and starts every day with an hour of meditation. He has a mysterious proximity to the divine without ever uttering the names of Gods. Hymns are written in his name, and regular folk bow to him as he passes by. And he feels nothing in return. He simply drags his sword along, probably stumbling into another situation which will later become legend."
		"npc_dota_hero_beastmaster_lore"					 "As The World Horn erupted in sound, a young boy was born at the foot of a Great Mountain. The echoes of the world horn are said to have imbued the boy with an immortal spirit. When war broke out, his father was forced to bring him along at just 8 years old since their armies were short on soldiers. The boy showed courage in war, but wasn’t very effective compared to his older allies that fought alongside him. During a battle with the Lightning Arc clan, one of their Chief Revenants fired a lethal bolt of lightning directly at our young warrior’s chest. Before it struck him, he felt a primal energy within that was waiting to burst. A case of ice surrounded him, surprising the nearby combatants and blocking the electric bolt. The ground began to shake beneath him, and raging fire began to swirl around the ice encased novice. Lady Gaia was watching him since the sound of the World Horn, and it is fabled that she is in fact his true mother. Suddenly the boy was destroying opposing soldiers with fireballs and earthquakes, and he became a champion of battle. At just 10 years old, he was feared across the entire continent and his tribe gave him the title of Elemental Warlord."
		"npc_dota_hero_leshrac_lore"						 "Once the powerful God of Judgement, Bahamut was ironically judged as unworthy by the Council of Lords due to his inability to cooperate. Although he was stripped of godhood and now stands among mortals to repent for his arrogance, he instead uses the powers he has left to continue dominating those he deems to be inferior. He is bound to roam the earth on an eternal quest of ascension, to become God once again."
		"npc_dota_hero_spirit_breaker_lore"				 "Risen from the very ashes of foundation, a beast of all worlds is what he called himself. He was a slave from birth. Bound to serve the ancients, his purpose is to bring darkness upon the world during times of war. He is engulfed with the blood of the deceased each time he runs over the battlefield’s horizon. He is neither alive nor dead, and most texts refer to him as a ghostly apparition whose sightings prophesize long periods of twilight."
		"npc_dota_hero_zuus_lore"						"When the Stones of the Ancients fell, a small Radiant Shard splintered into a singularity of sunshine and sparkling life. The stone sat dormant for 50 years, after which a single ray of Solus’s light shone upon it and sparked Auriun into being. Although Solus designed him to be perfect in every way, Auriun unexpectedly still carried the burden of original sin that plagued all mortals. Auriun can grant divine protection from all harm and even grant life, but can also produce eternal pain with the creeping shadow."
		"npc_dota_hero_templar_assassin_lore"			"She was born in the heart of Tanari jungle to a tribe of master hunters. Their traps were so proficient and deadly that nobody ever dared set foot on their territory. She was the youngest of the tribe, and so she gained the most experience by watching and learning from her elders. A terrible tragedy unfolded when her father died on the battlefield during the first awakening of Ancient God. For many years she studied techniques and the way of the assassin in order to become the ultimate predator. Stealth, traps, finesse, and cunning - all the tools a master killer needs. Now she seeks revenge on the spirit of Ancient God in memory of her once great father. She will one day fulfill her destiny to become the jungle's fiercest champion."
		"npc_dota_hero_huskar_lore"						"When he first heard legends of the Wind, Water, and Fire spirits of Tanari, he became obsessed with their stories and fearful of their power. He was determined to devote himself in totality to the Tanari spirits, and so he prayed and meditated every day in their honour. He practiced the art of war in order to defend the jungle from invaders and gain favour with the Spirits. One day during prayer in the depths of Tanari, a mystical tree that sheds its bark every one thousand years stood nearby. It is on this day that the 3 Tanari Spirits emerge from their temples and survey the jungle. The young warrior’s efforts had been noticed, and together the 3 Spirits blessed him with the spiritual essences of the Jungle, and he became the Spirit Warrior"
		"npc_dota_hero_legion_commander_lore"			"Born atop the highest peaks of Mt. Hyrath, she hails from an ancient civilization of mountain people. All members of this society are highly trained in combat from a young age in order to guarantee the continuation of their people. She became a champion when she stood atop the highest point of the mountain for 90 days without food or water and without moving. Her complete stillness began to turn her entire body into stone. After 90 days, she had become statue of solid rock. Most assumed she would stay that way forever, but 5 years later, she molted from the stone and upon her emergence she had the power to move mountains. She still defends her birthright to this day, and is known as the Mountain Protector."
		"npc_dota_hero_night_stalker_lore"				"漆黑的死神，黑暗的化身。他的起源是未知的，大多数关于他的故事都是基于猜测，根植于恐惧。他在黑夜中猎食，毫无偏见地杀人。有人说他可以变形，但也有人说他已经失去了变形的能力。传说，如果你看到一个蝙蝠翅膀的轮廓，你必须欺骗你的大脑，假装你什么也没看见。如果你让恐惧蔓延，你会发现你的心脏从你的胸部被瞬间撕裂。而你眼中最后看到的，只有永夜君王的轻蔑一笑"
		"npc_dota_hero_vengefulspirit_lore"				"One night over the village of Kul'el the skies were illuminated by black fire. Wherever it rained, crops would burn and houses were wrecked. As daylight finally dawned, a blazing comet descended from the sky. The sun itself was gone and the moonlight was no more. A child of the heavens was born. Named Solunia by the priest of the sun, she seemed no different than the average child. But on her 13th birthday she was brought into a fit of rage over a petty squabble. Her piercing screams seemingly splintered the sun and immediately brought on the night. After realizing what she had done, she began to cry, and to everyone’s amazement her tears shattered the moon and brought back the Sun. It became clear to the priests that Solunia was a special child of day and night and that her abilities should be brought under her control. Forced to balance the scales that tip over each day, Solunia has mastered her power and now wanders the planet imposing the force of the sun and the will of the moon wherever she feels it’s necessary."
		"npc_dota_hero_slardar_lore"						"Once a respected guardian of the Sea Fortress, Hydroxis fell from Siltbreaker’s good graces when he abandoned his duties to court a beautiful Naga maiden. The maiden ended up betraying him and stole valuable treasure from Val’Dun which she then brought back to her Kingdom. Despite being wrong, he enjoys his life as an exile and has chosen to go rogue, as he feels more comfortable when he owes no allegiance to anybody. Hydroxis still maintains dominion over his powers to manipulate water and sea, so he uses his time as an exiled guard practicing his command of the tides."
		"npc_dota_hero_visage_lore"						"Only the gloom of The Underworld could make creatures such as Ekkan thrive. Hell is a great place for the Soul Collector to dump the remains of the dead. Said to be an experimental puppet of Hades, Ekkan uses portals to travel between The Underworld and the mortal plane where he picks apart the bones of dead bodies. He breathes artificial life into useless corpses and takes control of the living and their souls to do his bidding. His motivations are unknown, seemingly Ekkan only has a drive to perpetuate the cycle of domination, killing, and reanimation."
		"npc_dota_hero_dark_seer_lore"					"Zhonik is the result of an extremely low probability quantum event where biological particles align in a cosmic accident which created a living being with no true origin. The unstable nature of his body grants him power over time and space, allowing him to run at incredible speeds despite his tiny misshapen legs. He can harness the power of surrounding particles and bend their existence in space-time to create unstable energy fields. Through his unique perceptions of the material world that cannot be understood by regular beings, he holds no allegiance or enmity to any person or group."
		"npc_dota_hero_antimage_lore"					"Arkimus was born into royalty as the son of the leader of the Lightning Arc clan. He was brutally trained by his father and his generals to be as powerful as his ancestors before. From a young age he would be forced to endure trials of lightning, where they would pass an electric current through his body with increasing voltage each time. Many children would die during these trials, but they were happy to let the electricity select for fitness. With the ability to manipulate lightning and arcane energy, Arkimus was able to slay his first dragon at the age of 13, which was an unprecedented feat. To the disappointment of his father, Arkimus did not wish to take the throne, and instead wanted to become a nomad who travelled and killed at will. His father started a fight with him, but Arkimus slaughtered the man for getting in his way. Arkimus left the brotherhood, leaving the the leadership of the Lightning Arc clan fractured and dismayed."
		"npc_dota_hero_monkey_king_lore"					"Residing in the highest treetops of Tanari, this mysterious beast learned to adapt to his surroundings and fend for himself like no other. As he has become one with nature, he can shapeshift into a multitude of different animal forms. Not much is known about him, he has no recognizable family and can take on a new appearance within seconds. He is known to appear in unexpected situations to defend any wildlife who is unfairly pursued by hunters."
		"npc_dota_hero_slark_lore"						"An enigmatic amphibious creature. He is a sneaky rascal and a thief. He was once trusted upon with the task of infiltrating Siltbreaker’s prison and setting him free. Slipfinn was successful, but upon his release Siltbreaker went on a rampage and slaughtered most of Slipfinn’s friends and family, among others. Its gratitude and mercy was only available for one being, Slipfinn, as a token of appreciation for setting him free, so he could rule over the Gloomy Depths again. Slipfinn set upon himself a task of redemption, to imprison the beast once more. He practices for this day by trapping beings in bubbles and mimicking their powers. He scours the world for clues, for treasures, curiosities and odds in hope to conquer the beast once more and put it back where it belongs."
		"npc_dota_hero_skywrath_mage_lore"				"Once a priest of Nefali, his fate has changed for worse ever since the temple was desecrated by the armies of Mak'Gorath. He took solitude on the eyries of the mountains, meditating and harnessing the powers of the wind. He became the first known Aeromancer, able to control the air and flows of wind with ease. Nefali finally granted him her blessing after many long years of solitude. A magical wind she said would protect him and those he holds dear from all harm. But he longed for more, so he continued his practice until he grew wings and was able to soar to the earth from the mountain tops. He received a jade staff from Nefali, enshrined with a gem that gleamed with the immense energy of tempests, which enchanted his winds to even greater levels. He had acquired all the power one could desire from Wind, which has the ability to protect and destroy. Finally he will have his revenge, and rebuild what was taken from him."
		"npc_dota_hero_winter_wyvern_lore"				""
